CONFLICT with T4 (2023 index OR = 09:30-10:00; the Core Content uses 09:30-10:30). Bot (off): `or60_bias`, `study.or60_study`. A pool counts as the stop run only when it was still untaken at 09:30, and an FVG only when it formed before the OR60 extreme.
  - Caption-gap fix (new-ipda-true-day; cc08_002 ~00:05:19: "every day at 3 P.M New York time ends the ipta true day"): the IPDA true day = 00:00-15:00 NY (the 15:00 bond close ends the daily range, cc08_001; a 16:00 index-close parameter is kept, `session_close`); inside it 03:00-10:00 NY is the high-volume window (cc12_004), not an alternative true day - prefer being positioned before 03:00 or in its first half; entries after the NY open get reduced objectives (a soft switch, not a filter). CONFLICT: the CC day-trade version (done by noon, FOMC days to ~15:00) against the last-hour trades of T10 / M5 (Ep 01; 032; Ep 08); a strict 03:00-10:00 filter would remove most bot entries (T5 09:30-11:00; T6 SB 10:00-11:00). Bot: `true_day="ipda"` (the table's daily candle from the 00:00-15:00 bars), `late_obj_k` (< 1: entries after 09:30 keep only the rungs within late_obj_k x ADR5), `cc_daytrade` (am_exit 12:00, PM off, FOMC days PM 14:00-15:00); the intraday 15:00 close / true-day range exist already (`dwc2.true_day`).
- **T13 Day templates.** Daily PO3: open, Judas move against the bias, expansion, close near the
  opposite extreme (Ep 04; cc08_008 ~1:34) [both]. CC six index profiles: two-session up/down close,
  AM rally/decline + PM reversal, two consolidation profiles (cc10_013; cc10_014); NFP days are
  "seek and destroy" exceptions [CC]. Classification needs an HTF array map (discretionary).
  - Caption-gap fix (T13-day-profile-selection; cc10_013 ~4:22; cc12_004 ~15:52: "when I'm bearish in the four hour has not yet traded to a discount array I expect New York open to continue lower"): selection criteria instead of 'discretionary': daily/4h IOF in the bias with no opposing 4h/daily/weekly array nearby = a two-session close in the bias direction; IOF in the bias but an opposing array 'very close' (within ADR5 [ours]) = the AM moves into the array, then a PM reversal; NY continues London's direction until a 4h opposing array is reached, then reverses. `day_profile` "tag", `day_profile_gate` (PM with the AM side on two-session days; the opposite once the array traded on reversal days).
  - Caption-gap fix (T13-judas-news-driver; cc01_002 ~15:16: "that's always going to come by way of some news event some news driver"): the manipulation (Judas) leg is usually triggered by a news driver released at the time of the move or just before it. Tag the Judas window to the nearest release (window = +-`judas_news_min` = 30 minutes [our interpretation]). Conflict: cc08_006 says no-news sessions are wildcards that can still be clean; N5: no 08:30 news = soft bias. Bot: `require_judas_news` (off; with `require_judas` only news-backed Judas sweeps count), `judas_news_tag` (off).

Bot (our mapping): AM longs off the discount array, PM longs after a lunch-low sweep or a discount-FVG tap, no shorts. DOWN close = the mirror. Valid only until price trades into an opposing 4h / daily / weekly PD array; then the AM-move / PM-reversal or consolidation profiles apply. Bot: `profile_two_session` (off; tags `two_session_up` / `two_session_down`; valid while no opposing 4h / D / W array was traded into anywhere between the AM swing origin and its extreme; the measured move PM leg ~= AM leg is an extra target from the entry), `two_session_catalyst` (off; on a scheduled-release day the lunch-sweep / discount trigger is dropped, tag `two_session_catalyst`), `two_session_am` (off; the AM: with the bias up and nothing opposing reached, AM orders are bias-direction only and at a same-side 1h+ array; tags `two_session_am_up` / `_down`).
  - Caption-gap fix (DS2-am-move-pm-reversal-templates; cc10_013 ~6:36-11:03): AM rally, PM reversal - daily / 4h IOF bullish but a 4h / daily premium array lies just above: the AM returns to discount then rallies into the HTF premium array; lunch consolidates; the PM runs the lunch-hour high (a lower high vs the AM high is a failure swing) OR the AM high, then reverses down into the close. Exception: if the AM rallied off a 4h / daily / weekly discount array the PM may instead retrace to it and resume higher; expect the reversal to carry through only when that AM array was lower-timeframe (60m / 15m). AM decline / PM reversal is the mirror. The trigger after the sweep is an MSS [our mapping]. Conflict with M6 (015 requires the intraday high to be taken). Bot: `pm_reversal_pool` "am_high" (as built) / "lunch_or_am", `pm_reversal_ltf_only` (off).
  - Caption-gap fix (DS2-consolidation-profiles; cc10_013 ~11:35 / ~12:00 / ~14:43: "especially if there's no real high impact or medium impact news expected during the 10 o'clock hour or later"): CONSOLIDATION profiles - preconditions: daily / 4h premium-discount location unclear (neutral IOF) AND no high- or medium-impact release in the 10:00 hour or later; not with a strong directional bias. AM rally / PM decline: the AM returns to a discount array then rallies (or expands up from the opening range's equilibrium to run London buy stops / the intraday highs); lunch consolidates; if the AM high reached a 1h / 4h+ premium array the PM runs only the lunch high, then reaches for the day's sell stops, otherwise the PM runs the intraday high, then the intraday / London sell stops; the PM may also just consolidate into the close. AM decline / PM rally is the mirror. Bot: `profile_consol_pm` (off; neutral bias [our interpretation] and no release from 10:00; the counter side only, sweep pool `lunch` or `am_session`, targets the day's / London's opposite extreme; tags `consol_am_rally` / `consol_am_decline`), `profile_consol_am` (off; the AM variants: neutral bias, no release from 10:00 - orders at an array of their side or, once the opening range has formed, from its EQ's expansion side; the day's / London's far extreme as targets; tags `consol_am_up` / `consol_am_down`). NOT built: 'the PM may also just consolidate into the close' (a possibility with no trigger).
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-consolidation-lunch-vs-am-extreme; cc10_013 ~13:28): consolidation (neutral order flow, no news), AM rally then PM decline: if the AM high traded into an HTF (1h / 4h+) premium array the PM raids only the LUNCH high, else the intraday (AM) high; then it reaches for the day's / intraday or London sell stops (alternative: the PM just consolidates into the close; mirror for AM decline then PM rally). Already built: `profile_consol_pm` (DS2-consolidation-profiles). Lunch raid reading (LLT3-lunch-hl-raid-not-target; cc10_013 ~1:50): the PM typically first runs the lunch-hour extreme opposite to its coming move (trending days may drop / rise into a discount / premium FVG instead; reversal and consolidation days may run the intraday extreme per the HTF filter), then targets the opposite liquidity (trending: continue into the close; reversal: reverse into the close; consolidation: the day's / London opposite stops). Bearish mirror: from 13:00 hunt the ES short-term high with SMT on the lunch high vs the post-13:00 high, hold at least to 15:00, ideally 16:00. Target: when the AM discount array was also HTF, expect only a bounce / consolidation there (cover); if not, hold for a break of the intraday low; hold toward 15:00-16:00. AM decline / PM reversal mirrors it (the intraday high is taken only if the AM premium array was not HTF).   - Caption-gap fix (T13-consolidation-profiles; cc10_014 ~00:14:28, ~00:17:47; X11 has only the ~14:00 exit): AM decline / PM rally - sell ES on bearish SMT at a premium array, hold 10:30-11:00; PM buy on SMT at the PM lows; when the AM low was at a 15m / 60m array nested in a 4h / daily array the PM low runs only the lunch low, else it first runs the AM low; the PM extreme typically forms around 14:00 at a 15m / 60m array - exit there; afterwards a drift back to the day's equilibrium; never hold to 16:00. The source uses the 0 GMT open, for indices test the midnight open [ours]. `po3_adr_invalid`: a low beyond open - ADR5 clears the long bias; the day summary reports (open - low) / ADR5.

Bearish (off a daily bearish breaker): after the midnight open a London rally above the open = Judas, sell short in London, expansion down into NY, reversal / retrace into the London close (10:00-12:00). Bullish (off a daily bullish breaker): the open trades down in London (Judas), go long, rally into the London close or the NY close. Needs the daily / 4h PD-array map. No Mon-Wed retracement into a discount array = skip the week [ours]. study_week.py reports impulse / retrace_into_array / expansion.

Mirror: Tuesday rises into an HTF premium array. Tag `tue_low` / `tue_high`.
  - Caption-gap fix (T14-wednesday-weekly-extreme; cc07_002 ~3:01, ~3:57; cc07_003 ~14:58): weekly bias bullish, neither Monday's nor Tuesday's low reaches the nearest HTF discount array: Wednesday London and / or NY drives into it (old M / W / D low sell stops, a broken-high retest, an M / W / D bullish OB) and forms the week's low (NY may undercut the London low once; the week's low is the lower of the two [ours]); after the sell stops go long, target the lower-TF premium array overlapping 1.27 / 1.68 of the Tue-Wed swing, reached Thursday or preferably Friday; mirror; tags `wed_low` / `wed_high`; optional `wed_week_extreme` (longs only that Wednesday).
  - Caption-gap fix (new-consolidation-thursday-reversal; cc07_002 ~4:51, ~5:41; cc07_003 ~24:47): weekly bias bullish, Mon-Wed a consolidation [ours: range <= 1.5 x ADR5, no daily close beyond the prior week's range], the HTF discount array below not yet reached: on Thursday an 08:30 employment release or a 14:00 FOMC / rate release drives price below the Mon-Wed low (a turtle-soup false break) into the array, then reverses; the Thursday low is the week's low; target the buy stops above the intra-week high, Thursday into Friday. Experienced-only entry: after the knee-jerk, a very small limit buy below the Mon / Tue low. The bot keeps N3 (no entries 14:00-14:45) and N2. Tags `thu_reversal` / `thu_reversal_bear` (`thu_reversal_tag`; `thu_reversal_trade` = the turtle-soup variant with the Mon-Wed low as the pool). Conflicts: the small FOMC knee-jerk entry vs N3; Thursday employment-release trading vs N2.
  - Caption-gap fix (new-consolidation-midweek-rally; cc07_002 ~6:40, ~7:20; cc07_003 ~26:43; stats only): weekly bias bullish, the last swing rallied from an HTF discount array and the premium target is not reached; Monday rallies (the Mon / Tue short-term high is the fulcrum), Tue-Wed retrace into a discount FVG / void / bullish OB, a high / medium release expected Wednesday, buy the discount array, target an M / W / D premium array overlapping 1.27 / 1.68 of the fulcrum-to-entry swing, price runs the intra-week high and expands into Friday; bearish mirror. Tags `midweek_rally` / `midweek_decline`.

Tag `wed_weekly_reversal`.
  - Caption-gap fix (DS2-weekly-template-sequence; cc09_008 ~41:53; cc12_003; stats only per M17): where the weekly high and low form vs the weekly bias, by weekday and session bucket (London 120-300, NY 510-660, PM 810-960): the bearish "Tuesday high + Thursday NY low" and the bullish "Mon / Tue / Wed low, high by Thursday 12:00" rates (study_week.py).
  - Caption-gap fix (DS2-weekend-first-driver; cc12_003 ~18:01: "there's going to be a slow start to the week because the economic calendar is rather quiet until around Tuesday New York open") [CC, discretionary; stats only]: at the weekend list the week's high / medium releases by day and session; hypothesis: with a quiet calendar until the first major driver the against-bias weekly extreme tends to form in that driver's session and the days before it are slow; re-evaluate daily; not systematic (~60 % confidence after Monday).

Typical sequence: Mon consolidation, Tue sell-off, reversal at the Tue / Wed overlap, buy Thu, delivery into the PBR Fri; mirror for bearish. study_week.py: weeks with a small Monday and a Tuesday sell-off > 1 x ADR5 -> does Thu / Fri return into Monday's range / exceed its high.

Study only: `study_week.week_state_labels` (consol / expansion / reversal per weekday [our interpretation], the weekday of the weekly high and low, the template count).

### 2.5 Levels and draw on liquidity

**Pools**
- **L1 Session pools** (highest high / lowest low of each window) [2023] (067 ~12:40-51:40):
  previous PM 13:30-16:00, London 02:00-05:00, NY lunch 12:00-13:30, AM 09:30-12:00, plus the ORG.
  Priority at 09:30: the previous PM range if price is near it; if already beyond, London's range;
  then the previous AM range (067 ~52:30). Bot AM sweep pools: PDL1-3, pre-market low (00:00/04:00
  to 09:30), REL; PM: AM-session H/L. Missing: previous PM range, London range, lunch range (P2).
  - Caption-gap fix (L1-rth-session-pools; 067 ~15:00, ~32:00-33:00, ~55:50): previous PM (13:30-16:00), AM (09:30-12:00) and lunch (12:00-13:30) ranges = the max high / min low of RTH bars in the window; London (02:00-05:00) = ETH bars. Overnight (16:00-09:30) prints never redefine an RTH session pool and an overnight trade beyond it does not retire it; the RTH pool stays the reference for the next RTH session (London stays spent by any print). Bot: `rth_pool_spent` (off): a previous PM / AM / lunch pool is spent only by prints from 09:30 today.
  - Caption-gap fix (L1-prior-day-london-pm-pools; 072 ~27:52: "we rip lower to take out the London session"): the previous day's London 02:00-05:00 high / low are pools next to today's London and the previous PM range; each stays a pool until traded through by a tick. Bot: `prev_london_pool` (off).
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-london-extreme-preny-range; cc08_007 ~2:37): at the NY open LR = [the London session extreme E (high when bearish, low when bullish), the opposite extreme printed between E and the NY open (09:30 for indices [our interpretation])]; bearish: the next trading day's retracement up into LR sells its premium half with an entry PD array; bullish mirror; also mark the prior NY session high / low - a retracement inside it often frames the next day's higher low (lower high). Bot: `london_lr_zone` (off; "tag" / "require"). Review fix: `ny_hl_frame` (off) marks the prior NY session (09:30-16:00) high / low - a retracement inside it frames the next higher low / lower high (tag in_ny_range); the premium-half test is up to E itself (a short above E is outside LR) [our interpretation].
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-0800-0900-pool-stock-open; cc10_006 ~4:06) [bonds-only; index carryover is our test]: the 08:00-09:00 high / low are liquidity pools that tend to be raided around the 09:30 stock open; for ES they are AM sweep pools (expect a raid of one edge at / after 09:30, then an M0 reversal). Bot: `or0809_pool` (off) = (480, 60) in `session_ors`.
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-consolidation-day-overnight-targets; cc10_008 ~00:16:19: "that's where your liquidity is going to be"): on a predicted consolidation day (N8) the NY targets are the overnight liquidity: London 02:00-05:00 high / low, the overnight high / low and the short-term swings formed overnight (low-resistance runs); both London extremes can be raided for AM opportunities; trade them in the AM only, done by 11:00; nearest-untaken selection is ours. Bot (off): `consol_overnight_targets`: on a DayPlan consol_day the only target is the nearest untaken overnight pool (single exit); PM or after 11:00 orders are skipped (x_consol_am_only).
- **L2 Liquidity matrix**: highs and lows of today, yesterday and the day before, plus the 3-day
  extreme (Ep 08; 010 ~65:54; cc09_005 ~14:22) [both]. PDH/PDL are the first daily targets and
  raid levels (006 ~37:12; cc04_011 ~12:57). An old daily high/low alone is not an entry: it needs a
  PD array there plus time of day (Ep 08; 010 ~63:38). Reversal at PDH/PDL needs an HTF array at
  the level and HTF order flow (cc09_005 ~03:12) [CC]. Bot: pdh1-3/pdl1-3 from RTH bars.

- **L9 FVG targets**: near edge first (an FVG beats an overlapping OB), then CE; CE of a weekly gap
  is enough, the far edge is a "ringer" (049 ~5:37; 061 ~2:30:15; 065B ~3:02:40) [2023]. A partly
  traded FVG's unvisited remainder plus the stops beyond it is the next draw (038 ~1:11:44). An
  unbroken OPPOSING HTF FVG between the entry and the target is resistance: it caps the trade (exit
  before its near edge, no target beyond it, no trade if the room is short) (Ep 02 ~2:15-5:22;
  owner correction #7). Bot: HTF arrays as targets (`htf_targets`); [owner #7 REVISED] the opposing FVG between price
  and the draw is a SPEED BUMP by default (`htf_resist_mode="bump"`: a partial at its near edge, the rest for the draw); `"cap"` = the
  first build (exit before it), `"split"` = cap on 1h+ and bump on 15m and lower. An FVG BEHIND the trade is never a target.

  fulcrum is traded through (Ep 06; 008 ~6:45; 065B). Use only the first SD level beyond the pool;
  skip levels at or before it; clusters of first-past-pool levels from several swings within 1-2

  - Caption-gap fix (new-rr-month-gate; cc02_001 ~00:12:11: "once you get one or two of them in your month ... you can now start lowering your risk to reward ratio"): until 1-2 big-payout winners (5R [our interpretation]) have closed this calendar month, require final-draw RR >= 5; after that setups with RR 2-3 are allowed if you want to stay busy. Bot: `rr_month_gate`, `rr_big`, `rr_n_big`, `rr_relaxed` (default off; compare with a fixed min RR).
  - Caption-gap fix (L15-chop-micro-scalps; 010 ~01:15:51: "that's a very very small little micro range but that's two and a half handles"): ICT can take ~2.5-handle micro scalps in chop (sell a retrace into a 1m FVG, target the nearest short-term low, two for ~5 handles) but prefers a 10-handle range before engaging; new traders study chop and do not trade it, seasoned traders turn charts off. Bot (off, experienced): `chop_scalp_pts` (with `sloppy_mode="scalp"`: the scalp's only exit is the pool within that distance, which is also its minimum room; entries only at an FVG retrace), `chop_scalp_max` 2, `chop_scalp_day_pts` 5.
  - Caption-gap fix (L15-5-handle-legs; 009 ~17:52: "each one will potentially accumulate to 15 handles"): count completed 5-ES-pt legs in the trade direction (reporting / partials); ~3 legs (~15 handles) is what one day can accumulate (009, one example). A leg = a run of >= 5 pts from a pivot low to a pivot high, no fixed price grid [ours]. Reported as `legs5_bias` in the day summary; no trading effect.
  - Caption-gap fix (L15 / M3; 054 ~0:36-1:45, 059 ~20:34, 058 ~54:20): the framework is the best-case delivery to and through the draw, not the entry-to-exit range (059: stops at 4120, ~3 handles beyond the pool count); room counts only to the nearest untaken pool or the near edge of an opposing FVG, never past a low already swept today. Bot: `sb_room_overshoot_pts` (0; test 3), `sb_room_untaken`, `sb_org_chop` (see M3).
- **L16 ADR5** = 5-day average daily range [CC]: expected day range; aim to capture 65-70% of it;
  ADR reached before 10:00 -> take 80% and keep a runner (cc08_001 ~1:53; cc09_006 ~19:33). Also
  N8 filters. {1m}
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-judas-depth-third-adr; cc09_008 ~46:50): FX classic day - the move from the midnight NY open against the bias reaches roughly 1/3 of ADR (33 pips on a 100-pip day); the LOD candidate zone is [open - ~0.33 x ADR5, open], ideally a 15m discount array converging with a daily / 4h / 60m one. Index use and the weakening of a deeper dip are our interpretation. Already built: `judas_max_adr` (0.33 / 0.5) and `judas_depth_tag` (B12-judas-depth-adr) - no new switch.
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-adr5-target-ladder; cc09_006 ~00:19:23, cc09_007 ~00:08:32, cc12_004 ~00:17:45: "targets will be the five-day average daily Range High and the next higher time frame premium array"): ADR5 = mean(high - low) over the last 5 days. Long: ADR_high = the day low or the London low + ADR5; T1 = ADR_high, T2 = the next 4h or 60m premium PD array above (cc09_006); reached before 10:00: 80 % and a runner; beyond ADR_high: fib the ADR range and use the 1.27 and 1.62 extensions only where a 15m / 60m premium array overlaps (cc12_004, extends L13). Shorts mirror. Treating the first-reached as the main exit is ours. Bot (off): `adr_t2_htf`, `adr_ext` (within 1 pt of a 15m / 1h array), `adr_anchor` day_low / london_low (the `adr_target` rung).
  - Caption-gap fix (L16-adr5-60-80-closed; cc08_007 ~00:17:25: "take or have had taken 60 to 80 percent off at five day average daily range projections"): unconditional (not only before 10:00; the earlier-time note stays cc08_001): when price reaches the ADR5 projection (from the day's low for longs, the high for shorts - ours) 60-80 % of the position is closed in total and the rest runs for later in the day. Bot: `adr_closed_min` (0 = off; 0.7; with `adr_early` for the pre-10:00 80 %).
  - Caption-gap fix (LLT3-adr-fib-close-zone; cc09_006 ~26:59: "close in between the 20 and 30 levels on your FIB") [FX source; index use untested]: bullish day: L = the day low or London low, H = L + ADR5 anchored ahead of time; the expected close is on H, or after trading off H in [H - 0.30 R, H - 0.20 R] (R = ADR5); the London-close counter-trade (10:30-13:00, after ADR is met) targets a 20-30 % retracement of the day's range (cc09_007); bearish mirror. `close_in_adr_zone` is reported first; `adr_close_runner` exits the runner at H - 0.20 R after price traded H and a bar closed back below it.
  - Caption-gap fix (L16-adr-contraction-runner; cc09_008 ~47:45: "we probably caught a tiger by the tail and we need to leave something on"): (a) flag: ADR5 contracted (FX < 60 pips; index [ours]: the bottom tercile of the last 60 sessions' ADR5) -> expect a large-range day soon in the IOF direction; a context tag (`adr_contracted_tag`), not a trigger. (b) trigger: price already covered >= ADR5 in the IOF direction by the NY open (index [ours] 09:30): bank most of the position and keep a runner exempt from the 12:00 am_exit, allowed to run to 13:00-14:00 (`adr_met_runner`, `adr_runner_until`). Conflict: D16 am_exit = 12:00 vs cc09_008's 13:00-14:00 runner.
- **L17 Rounding**: CC rounds every level toward price to the nearest round number (cc12_001
  ~34:00); 2023 index work does not (007 is the one case). Bot: `round_step_pts` switch (off):
  targets rounded down and limit entries up toward price on the real front-month price (#13
  mapping); stops not rounded.
- **L18 Initial FVG references** (N-initial-fvg-reference; 048 ~5:40, 082 ~5:13) [2023]: reference arrays, tagged respected / disrespected later; confluence and a narrative tag only, never a stand-alone entry. `initial_fvg_day` = the first 5m FVG whose third candle closes 09:30-10:00, extended to 16:00; `initial_fvg_week` = the first FVG of Monday's session (048: a 5m FVG at Monday 14:50) [our interpretation: the week's first trading session], extended to Friday's close; `monday_10am_1h_fvg` = if Monday's 10:00 1h candle is c2 of a 1h FVG, its high / low / CE as a weekly array (082: a BISI used as resistance later in the week). Bot: `initial_fvg` (empty; "day" / "week" / "mon10"); `Trade.init_fvg_respected`.
- **L19 Graduated pricing / second touch** (X3-graduated-pricing-second-touch; cc04_010 ~11:24: "it can't be done on the first pass"): institutional repricing is graduated - after price runs away from a level the return often tests it more than once (the second pass may poke a few ticks beyond); a second test followed by bearish candles that leave a gap / void = a sell limit inside that gap (mirror for buys). A confluence TAG from one CC example, not a mandatory touch count. Bot: `touch_tag` (off; `Trade.touch_n` = the separate approaches to the swept level within 4 x `eq_tol_pts` of it since it formed, the sweep included).

### 2.6 Models and setups

- **M0 The common skeleton ("one setup for life")** [2023] (067 ~12:00-26:00, ~1:30:00; 063
  ~12:22; 065A ~2:28:00; cc12_004 ~26:28):
  1. Bias = what the weekly/daily candle reaches for; one direction only.
  2. Pick a time window and mark its high and low pools (L1).
  3. Wait for a raid on the pool against the bias (the Judas).
  4. A structure shift with displacement leaves an FVG.
  5. Enter on any PD array (FVG, OB, breaker, OTE, IFVG, turtle soup, wick CE).
  6. Target the opposite pool or gap.
  Not every condition is in the bot (no HTF bias; see P3).
  - Caption-gap fix (NEW-low-of-day-zone-tag-first; 093 ~8:46: "it has to drop down to that level first"): when a pre-market low-of-day level is defined (093: the CE of an hourly candle's tail, with the 5m discount FVG, a breaker and the daily inversion-FVG levels beneath), take no longs until price trades down to it; then require SMT + MSS + FVG on the lower timeframe. Mirror for a high of day. Bot: `lod_zone_first` (off; [our interpretation] the zone = the nearest completed 60m candle tail (a lower wick of at least 0.1 x ADR5) CE below the 08:30 price with a 5m bull FVG - or a daily array - within `eq_tol_pts`; no long until a bar's low <= zone + 2 ticks, then the sweep needs the SMT tag).
  - Caption-gap fix (new-delivery-states-machine / new-state-to-array-map; cc01_001 ~2:33 / ~7:51, cc01_002 ~12:42 / ~18:35): label the current leg CONSOLIDATION (a clear range) / EXPANSION (a quick move away from the range EQ) / RETRACEMENT (back inside the recent range) / REVERSAL (a run just beyond an old high / low, then rejection); transitions C -> E only; E -> Ret or Rev, eventually C (cc01_001 also allows E -> C and Rev -> Ret; cc01_002 forbids C -> E -> C). Bot: no entry in CONSOLIDATION. Tool map: EXPANSION <-> order blocks (wait for the return to the OB near EQ), RETRACEMENT <-> FVG / void / VI inside the recent range, REVERSAL <-> a liquidity pool / stop run beyond an old extreme, CONSOLIDATION <-> EQ (wait for the impulse away); specialising in one pair is acceptable. Bot: `levels.delivery_state` (thresholds [our interpretation]), `state_machine` (off; helper of the DWC package built here), `consol_bars` (20), `consol_adr` (0.3), `state_tool_map` (off; entry kinds outside the map pass). [Second pass day_week_context#2/3 (definitions_pd_arrays_structure #125): already built - `state_machine`, `consol_bars`, `consol_adr`, levels.delivery_state, sm2 gate; no duplicate switch.]
  - Caption-gap fix (new-divergence-types; cc04_013 ~2:41 / ~12:15 / ~13:52): type 1 (classic) = price higher high, momentum lower high - the retail reversal signal, often trapped (expect a stop run into an OB, then continuation the other way); type 2 (hidden) = price higher low, momentum lower low = continuation above the recent high. No overbought / oversold reading needed; confirmation only, never a trigger. Mirrors. Bot: `div_tag` (off; `Trade.div_type` "type1" / "type2" from the last two swing lows and the 14-bar stochastic %K, `levels.stoch_divergence`).
  - Caption-gap fix (new-consolidation-exit-direction; cc09_004 ~5:07: "that's going to be the direction of the move outside the consolidation"): a lower-timeframe consolidation most often exits in the direction of daily / 4h order flow; a move out of the range against that flow is a stop run to fade - expect a return through the EQ to the opposite extreme and just beyond; use the HTF-side pool as the draw. Bot: `consol_fade` (off; a wick out of a CONSOLIDATION range against the bias that closes back inside: a limit at the range edge, stop beyond the break extreme, target the opposite extreme).
  - Caption-gap fix (new-price-engine-models; cc09_006 ~0:54, cc09_007 ~1:27 / ~2:23): for a bullish HTF IOF: OA = a sweep of a key intraday old low, then a long to a short-term premium array; RA = (often after a sell-stop raid) a retrace into a discount fair-value array (typically OTE), long there. Bearish: OD (buy-stop run, then a short to a discount array) and RD (retrace up into a premium array, short). All are scalps in the London / NY kill zones. Bot: `Trade.setup_model` in {OA, RA, OD, RD} (always tagged: a sweep-driven order = OA / OD, a non-sweep array retrace = RA / RD).
  - Caption-gap new rule (new-falling-dagger-upper-half-buy; 012 ~00:20:06) - EXPERIENCED-ONLY, off by default; CONFLICT with E1 (never chase) and M0 (sweep and MSS first), Section 3 decision 26: with a bullish HTF bias and a daily BISI that price is trading back into, the reaction should come in its UPPER half (above the daily CE) and the lower-half imbalances (the hourly FVGs there) stay open. A pre-MSS buy limit into the falling move in the upper half (our interpretation: at the daily CE, or the highest 15m / 60m FVG low between the CE and the daily top), stop just under the daily CE; skip once price filled a lower-half imbalance. Bot (off): `dagger_entry`, `dagger_level` "ce" / "ltf".
  - Caption-gap fix (M0-all-nearby-pools-run-first; 069 ~00:16:49: "the reason why I wanted to wait and I didn't take this one here"): step 3 preference / quality filter - before a counter-Judas entry note the session pools on the Judas side (London high, the prior-day NY session high) and prefer to wait until ALL of them are run, with price above the midnight open and into a premium array; an MSS after only the nearer pool is lower quality. Bot: `all_pools_first` "tag" / "require" (off; `all_pools_names` london / prev_ny, `all_pools_pts` 30 from the open).
  - Caption-gap new rule (new-next-day-or-reh-short; 099 ~00:52:08: "if they stay like this and we don't break this low overnight I would expect this run here tomorrow morning"): next-day plan template - the prior day drove down into the low end of an HTF inefficiency (discount) and the overnight does not trade under the prior day's low: mark the prior day's 15m relative equal highs (the trailed buy stops), expect the AM run above them (no specific window), after the sweep an MSS lower with an FVG, short that FVG, target the low that formed before the sweep, stop above the sweep high [ours]; cancel if the prior low breaks overnight. Mirror. Bot: `prev_reh_plan` (off; the REH pools join the AM pools from 09:30, the target is the last swing before the sweep).
  - Caption-gap fix (M0-htf-array-not-reached; 103 ~00:29:15: "I would have liked to seen it trade back up into this inefficiency ... but it was unwilling to get there"): if the planned HTF entry array (e.g. a 4h IFVG) is not reached and price stalls below it, keep the HTF draw unchanged, drop to lower TFs (4h -> 1h -> 15m) and enter at the bearish breaker formed under the unreached array, still above the draw; adds inside the breaker candle; stop above the breaker high, trailed; an FVG as the alternative entry is ours. Mirror. Bot: `array_fallback_breaker` (off): the 15m breaker between the unreached 1h / 4h array and the locked draw arms a breaker order.
  - Caption-gap fix (M0-judas-ends-in-15m-array; cc09_001 ~00:05:11) [FX; the index use is ours, a preference, not a requirement]: step 3 - price declines under the NY midnight open and the Asian low, ideally into a logical 15m discount array (bullish OB / FVG / old low); price should leave that array sharply - if it hovers there the odds drop and the setup is downgraded or skipped. Shorts mirror. Bot: `sweep_15m_array` "tag" / "require" (off), `sweep_leave_bars` / `sweep_leave_pts` (0 = off; the sweep is dropped when price stays within the points of its extreme for more than N bars).
  - Caption-gap fix (OT-one-state-tool-pairing; cc01_001 ~00:19:34: "you need know what context or framework you're going to trade in couple that with an ICT tool and then wait for those conditions"): pick ONE market condition (consolidation / expansion / retracement / reversal) coupled with ONE tool, trade only that, and add others over time; backtest each pairing alone before combining (the four pairing names and the state classifier are ours). Bot (off): `pairing` "expansion_ob" / "retracement_void" / "reversal_pool" / "consolidation_eq" (an order passes only when the session state AND the entry kind match; `Trade.state`; `state_tag` stamps the state on every trade with pairing "all" so the report can split by it).
  - Caption-gap fix (M0-target-opposite-extreme-same-range; 067 ~00:18:56: "why because it's inside the range between 130 and 4:00"), step 6: the first target = the other end of the raided range - after price raids one extreme of a session range against the bias (prev PM 13:30-16:00, London 02:00-05:00, lunch 12:00-13:30) and an MSS + FVG forms, T1 = the opposite extreme of that same range. Bot: `same_range_t1`.
  - Caption-gap fix (OT-pre-session-checklist-order; cc12_004 ~22:21: "I'm telling you in the order of the very specific detail of the order of how I do each thing"): pre-session checklist, in ICT's order: 1) HTF monthly / weekly / daily bias (4H view); 2) day of week and the expected weekly / daily template (Mon-Wed preferred; Thu / Fri templates if Mon-Wed fails); 3) IPDA true-day hours (bulk 03:00-10:00 NY); 4) kill zone; 5-7) CBDR, Asian range and flout SDs [fx-only]; 8) the intraday profile (London / NY continuation vs reversal); 9) key PD-array levels, internal vs external range, rounded to the nearest 10 / 5; 10) ADR5 high / low with the 127 / 162 extensions overlapping 15m / 60m arrays. Look for a setup only when every step is green; filters veto first (OT-filters-before-bias). ICT notes the order is how he works, not an invitation never to experiment; the list is FX / London-heavy. Bot (off): `checklist` "tag" (Trade.cl_fail) | "gate" (+ `cl_days`, `cl_pm_ok`): htf, dow, ipda, kz, profile, levels, adr (steps 5-7 are not computed).
- **M1 AM market maker buy/sell model (MMBM/MMSM)** - the bot's `am_mm` [2023] (Ep 03; Ep 05;
  Ep 07; 003 ~10:38-16:45; 009 ~1:00-16:19):
  - Context: HTF draw on the far side (daily VI, REH); price drops into a discount array (5m OB / 1m
    BISI).
  - Trigger: a short-term low (the pool) is taken, then the swing high that led into the low breaks
    with displacement, leaving an FVG (A19).
  - Speed check: price must "rip through" the small SIBI high; afterwards it must not trade below
    the lowest open or close of the swing low (009 ~15:51-16:19).
  - Entry: limit at the FVG (unicorn FVG preferred, A14). 003 took its first entry in the OB before
    the MSS (D10).
  - Stop: below the MT of the bullish OB nearest the sweep low (009 ~4:17) (E5).
  - Full MM model phases (original consolidation, distribution, smart money reversal, re-accumulation,
    return to the original consolidation's buy side) are context for where you are, not extra
    triggers (003 ~1:27:50; 104 ~27:28-53:35; cc07_007). If you missed a stage, the next one
    becomes your first (104 ~37:12).
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-original-consolidation; 104 ~27:53): the original consolidation (bullish example): upper bound = a smooth-edge level that bodies / highs touch repeatedly ("a repetitive overlapping or reoccurrence of a level"); lower bound = the range's lowest low; bearish mirror. [our interpretation] >= 3 body tops / highs within 0.5 ES pt inside the bars around the parent swing. Bot: `orig_consol` (off) with `orig_consol_touches` (3) and `orig_consol_tol_pts` (0.5): `mmxm_ctx` draws to that level instead of the parent swing high.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-turning-level-types; cc04_007 ~1:04): the MM buy model's turning level is any higher-TF or immediate-term support: an old high (as support), an old low, a bullish OB, a liquidity void being filled or an FVG being closed into; the buy side begins only once price reaches one (mirror for the sell model). Bot (off by default = the first build): `mm_turn_types` ("ob", "fvg" + "old_high", "old_low", "void").
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-three-entry-plans; 003 ~1:27:30-1:29:00): three equally valid ways to trade the MM buy model, "a matter of preference", each a separate test: "full" (the low-risk buy at the smart-money reversal, OB / BISI after the short-term-low sweep, hold to the original consolidation's buy side), "mss_scalp" (wait for the MSS, buy the retrace into its FVG, exit at the first relative equal highs), "pyramid" (buy at the low, add on each re-accumulation FVG that holds, scale out at each pool). Exit level per plan is our interpretation. Bot (off): `mm_plan`.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-sell-model-fail-to-reach-CE; 007 ~20:36, moved from the Ep 05 appendix): sell model - (1) price rallies into an HTF (1h) SIBI and forms REH near its high end, (2) a down move breaks the swing low (MSS) leaving an FVG / SIBI, then a second MSS lower, (3) on the next rally into that SIBI the candle whose high FAILS to reach the SIBI CE is the sell, at market on that candle; targets the sell side under the original consolidation, then lower imbalances / the NWOG; stop per E6 [our interpretation]. Long = mirror. Bot (off): `ce_fail_entry`.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-inside-fvg-expansion-only; 009 ~3:49, ~5:12): context note - when the day's objective is ONE pre-named fixed level (an old daily close) and a single expansion rather than a trend day is expected, the "advanced" inside-FVG model is used: enter inside the FVG toward its middle after the 09:30 sell-side purge, aim at that level, take the bulk off at the level or higher (9 of 14). ICT gives no rule for trend days. [our interpretation: single expansion = the lock is a single daily level within ADR5 of the open; bulk 65%.] Bot (off): `expansion_ce_entry`, `expansion_bulk`.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-original-consolidation-entry; 104 ~00:32:10: "it doesn't have to touch it but if it does that's usually a trade"): MM buy model - price has left the original consolidation when a candle BODY closes above its high (wicks do not count); a later trade back down to that high = go long there (the "original entry"); if it never returns use the next higher-order entry (the next PD array of the later stages). Mirror. Bot: `mm_oc_entry` (off; stop under the consolidation midpoint [ours]).
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-consolidate-at-discount-array; 104 ~00:23:42: "we would be anticipating some measure of consolidation not dropping lower"): after the hourly shift and the retrace into the discount FVG / OB: sideways inside / at the array keeps the long thesis toward BSL; a drop through it = the leg is not confirmed [our interpretation: N bars with a range under X inside the array valid, a body close under the array low invalid]. Bot: `mm_accum_bars` / `mm_accum_frac` (off): arm a limit at the array CE after N consecutive bars inside it whose total range is under the fraction of its height; a close under its low cancels. Review round: `mm_accum_gate` makes the accumulation test a gate on the other MM-model orders (original-consolidation, stages) instead of only a standalone order.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-accumulation-stages; 104 ~00:36:46: "there's your entry for a first stage accumulation"): stages are counted from the trader's OWN first entry - the return to the original consolidation = the original entry; the breaker OB (the down-close candle inside a bullish breaker) = 1st-stage accumulation (or the 2nd if the original entry was taken); a later BISI retraced only to its CE = the next stage (reaccumulation); each valid only while the BSL / HTF target above is untaken; pyramid from stage to stage. Mirror. Bot: `mm_stages` (off): flat = the first stage is the entry, after a fill the next stages arm adds (E12 sizes), none once the lock is delivered.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-distribution-stages-lowrisk-short; 104 ~00:45:30, ~00:49:45: "that bearish order block is your second stage redistribution"): reversal - price wicks above the 60m SIBI while bodies stay inside it, then breaks lower: (0) low-risk short = a rally back to the 60m SIBI CE, stop just above the reversal highs (a stop-out re-enters on a later touch of the FVG low); (1) stage 1 distribution = the bearish breaker; (2) stage 2 redistribution = the next rally into a bearish OB (to its MT) or into the body of a bullish OB on the buy side of the curve - the fastest leg, if missed do not chase; ~80% off at the original consolidation. Mirror for the buy model. Bot: `mm_lowrisk` (+ `mm_oc_frac` 0.8, off) and `mm_stages` for the stage entries; the stop-out re-entry and the stage-2 OB-MT variant are NOT built (the BISI CE is the stage 2 entry). Review round: the 60m-SIBI wick pattern no longer needs a remembered same-side sweep (only the original-consolidation partial does); `mm_lowrisk_reentry` ("extreme" / "wick_ce") adds the one re-entry at the FVG's near edge after the first stop was run; `mm_stage2` adds the stage-2 entry (a retrace into the MT of an order block of the post-MSS move, or the body of a reclaimed block) and never chases a stage-2 leg whose entry price already traded.
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-one-segment; 104 ~00:09:27: "they can either trade the long side of the side of the curve or they can sell short on the cell side of the curve"): implement each MM leg as its own setup (the buy-side leg: original / stage entries to the HTF target; the sell-side leg: low-risk short / stage entries to under the original consolidation); reversing from the long into a short at the target is advanced and OFF by default. Bot: `mm_flip` (off): the session reopens for the opposite side after an MM trade reached its last level. Review round: the flipped leg is an MM leg only (orders with sm5 kind "mm": mm_lowrisk, stage, original-consolidation, accumulation orders).
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-short-sell-side-of-buy-curve; cc04_007 ~00:01:21: "you can anticipate it and watch it go lower and be short") - A18 / M1: if an MMBM is anticipated (HTF draw above, an untagged HTF support below) a short on the sell side of the curve toward that support is allowed, targeting the support; ICT gives no entry model - use the bot's normal bearish entries, exit at the support; switching to longs at the reclaimed blocks after a higher high is a separate setup, doing both on one curve is advanced (off); if the decline already happened skip the short and only map the reclaimed blocks. Bot: `curve_short` (off; the draw up + an untouched HTF discount array below: the short side is scanned against the bias, its single target the array's near edge).
  - Caption-gap fix (M1-intraweek-pbr-target; cc07_007 ~18:06): weekly-profile context only (M17 keeps it as statistics / research) - an intraweek reversal's objective is a return into the PBR (see T14); near / far-edge partials are the bot's proxy.
- **M2 2022 model** [2023] (022 ~38:38-1:03:08; 042 ~34:53-1:09:17; 062 ~34:53; 074; 082; 087;
  094):
  - Short: buy-side (old high / REH / NWOG / London high) is taken, then displacement breaks a
    short-term low (MSS).
  - Sell an FVG inside the displacement leg (high -> the low that broke) that sits at or above 50%
    of the leg (premium). Never sell the breakout candle itself.
  - FVG choice: one aligned with a VI and bearish OB; with two FVGs the higher one is allowed;
    otherwise the first FVG above EQ (022 ~55:00; 042 ~47:20; 082 ~5:31).
  - The FVG must close, a new candle open, and price trade back in without first taking the low
    (042 ~34:53).
  - Stop: just above the bearish OB MT (022 ~59:02, 2 pts), above the FVG candle high (062 ~37:28),
    or above the CE of the higher of two FVGs (069 ~13:46).
  - Pyramid only in premium; past the leg's 25% level, stop adding and only take partials (022
    ~49:26; 025 ~47:56). Target sell-side below 50% of the range, preferring the larger pool (042

- **M3 Silver Bullet (SB)** [2023] - his main setup from file 044 on (044; 047; 048; 054; 057; 058;
  059; 060; 063; 065A; 066; 068; 090; 092; 099):

  - In the window, displacement toward the draw leaves an FVG facing the trade (a BISI for longs);
    enter on the retrace into it; target the opposing untapped pool (044 ~1:06:34; 048).
  - The first FVG in the bias direction during the hour is the entry; it must sit beyond the level
    that needed breaking (048 ~57:36, ~1:34:31). An MSS before the FVG is shown (054; 055; 058) but
    never stated as required. Demonstrated premises (optional filters, `sb_premise`): a tag of a 15m
    discount FVG then MSS (054 PM), or ES/NQ SMT at the predefined level then MSS (093).
  - Evolving SB: if price leaves the first FVG without a retrace, move the limit to the next FVG in
    the window; better to miss than chase (057 ~2:16; 058 ~46:43).
  - Cancel if price trades ~3 ticks beyond the first draw before the fill (057 ~3:35: 4203 vs a
    4203.75 draw); a 1-2 tick run beyond it is wanted (057 ~4:18). Bot: `sb_cancel_ticks`.
  - Room: >= 10 ES pts to the draw, take ~5 or cover at the old low (054; 058; 059; 060); looser
    variants accept >5 (044) or 4-5 (068; 072).
  - Entry price: limit near the FVG near edge (044; 057), CE as "worst case" (048).
  - Stop (rarely stated): 2 pts above entry / top of the evolved FVG (057 ~6:41); below the candle
    forming the FVG low and below the OB MT (090 ~2:14:00); below the entry candle (086 ~11:35).
  - Session order: London, else AM, else PM; one fires every day in one market; if not in your
    market, don't switch (054 ~18:18; 046 ~55:21; 090). One AM SB a day is a preference, not a cap (047 ~8:00 "if I was only going to trade one time a
    day in the morning" - then choose 10:00-11:00; `one_am_trade_mode="sb_only"`, needs `sb_model`).
  - An immediate rebalance seen in 10:00-11:00 -> expect more than 5 handles (048 ~39:02). Bot:
    `sb_rebalance_ext` switch (off): with an A7 immediate rebalance in the window the SB draw must

  - Exits: layered limits (057: 4/2/4 of 10 at NWOG high/75%/CE); with the weekly profile aligned,
    most off at target and keep 1 runner (048 ~2:02:12).
  - Caption-gap new rule (new-shallow-rel-sweep-one-more-drive; 060 ~59:18): if price trades only a few ticks under an REL and bounces, the sell-side run is INCOMPLETE - no long; expect a retrace into the nearest FVG / mitigation block, then one more drive lower that clears the stops under the REL and the lows below; look for longs only after that deeper run plus an MSS and an FVG / OB entry. Limit at the near edge, or market on a down-close candle into it (076). Stop below the entry candle's low or beyond the breaker. Room (L15) applies. The full size can be two resting limits (057 ~1:46): ~60% one tick inside the near edge, ~40% two ticks shallower (it fills first), one stop, both move with an evolved SB (`sb_split_limits`).
    * Count: while the draw is untaken, a return into the filled SB FVG re-enters / adds (decreasing size, E12) and a new FVG / VI on the next leg is an IOFED add (080); PM gives two chances, plus one in 15:15-15:45 if a target is unengaged (056) (`sb_max_per_window`, `sb_adds`, `sb_moc_extra`). After a qualified SB reaches an HTF array and its entry FVG inverts, an opposite "evolving" SB in the same window may form (058 ~44-47, illustrative; MSS beyond the run's origin, a new FVG, room; `sb_reverse_after_target`). A same-direction SB fill during an open 2022-model trade is a pyramid add, not a new trade (058 ~43:10), below the EQ of the sweep..draw range and before any partial (`sb_as_add`).
    * Immediate rebalance in the SB window (A7): usually no retrace fill (runaway), do not chase under SB rules; a standard FVG: take the simple SB to its target and be done for the AM (048 ~1:35; `sb_imm_tag`, `sb_am_done`).
    * Exits: supercharge = daily not ranging AND Monday-Wednesday AND the week's low (high) not yet clearly in AND the weekly profile aligned AND the seasonal tendency agrees: most off at the SB target, one runner to the HTF draw, no breakeven; otherwise the full exit at the target (048 ~2:02; `sb_supercharge`, `sb_supercharge_seasonal`). Over-zealous T2 (047 ~8:26): the near edge of the HTF imbalance (1h FVG) beyond the opposing pool, held after the window until the AM close (`sb_t2`, `sb_t2_tf`). Trailed stops are run after the AM SB target too: expect a quick run back toward the upper quadrant (75%) of the daily discount FVG before the larger move (055 ~14:35) - a tight trail under the SB leg is likely to be hit (`sb_runner_trail` "none", study tag `sb_flush_tag`). Unmanaged (`sb_mgmt` "fixed") vs managed day trade (069 ~15:57; `sb_mgmt` "day_trade"): the day trade adds on the next FVG / SIBI of the displacement (E12 sizes), is held through lunch and targets the deeper NWOG CE.
    * Draw / room: the NWOG draw is a return to the current or any older retained NWOG, or an expansion away from one after price reacts at it (054 ~5:17; `sb_nwog_expansion`). Room counts only to the nearest untaken pool or the near edge of an opposing FVG, never past a low already swept today; price consolidating inside today's ORG at the window = not a classic SB (058 ~54:43; `sb_room_untaken`, `sb_org_chop`, `sb_fail_mode`). The 10-handle (and 5-handle) framework is the best-case run, measured to the pool plus the expected run through it (059: stops at 4120, ~3 handles beyond counts), not to the exit price; the exit stays at the pool / ~5 pts (`sb_room_overshoot_pts`).
- **M4 Breaker and unicorn entries** [both]:
  - Classic: buy the first return to the bullish breaker; bodies respect it; stop beyond the sweep
    extreme (A13; cc05_005 ~11:06).
  - Unicorn: buy the FVG/CE inside the breaker right after a sell-side run at 09:30; add on each new
    imbalance; accumulate inside the breaker before the breakout, not on the breakout (Ep 05; 007
    ~22:39-23:35). 018: may enter the breaker but not below its MT.
  - Advanced (070 ~34:59-1:08:31): use only the A->B leg; with bias on side you may buy inside the
    A-B leg or on the sell-side run without waiting for the break; a fill at MT needs 1 tick through;
    target B + 1x(B-A). Best when the lower low also hits an HTF pool.
  - Pyramid: buy the turtle-soup low, add at the breaker retest (cc12_004 ~33:23; 063 ~28:37).
  - Breaker swing vs failure swing (CC): one more drive into the key level then a break of the
    in-between low (breaker), or a lower high that fails to reach it (failure swing); sell the retest
    of the breaking point; stop above the new/failed high. Both levels must be drawn before price
    arrives (cc05_005) [CC].

Bot (off): `breaker_need_bias_draw`.

    the 60m FVG edge, then a fast candle leaves a breakaway FVG; buy the top of the 1m FVG; add in
    the FVG, the breakaway gap and on a breaker; stop below the FVG candle open, then trailed; one
    exit at the draw (001 ~39:03-45:20). Price should hold above the midpoint of the 4h FVG (Ep 01
    ~30:48).

- **M13 OTE** [CC] (cc01_004 ~9:44-53:45; cc01_005 ~2:07-20:47): impulse leg from a confirmed swing
  low to the high; buy only below 50%; zone 62-79%, sweet spot 70.5%; never AT 50%; stop beyond the
  leg's swing low; no retrace to 50% = no trade; target above the old short-term high or just before
  an opposing OB. 2023 uses OTE as one entry option among many (065A); 061 says fib only for
  premium/discount, never entries. cc12_004 pattern 1 ("favorite"): OTE + FVG + swept short-term
  low + bullish OB, target external liquidity. D12.

  - Friday only, after a trending one-sided week that reached an HTF premium (bullish week) or

Mirror for bearish. `tgif_strict`, `tgif_weeks`.
  - Zone: retrace 20-30% of the weekly range (week low incl. Sunday 18:00 to the high) from the
    extreme; 25% = midpoint; 40%+ is reversal talk.
  - Timing: weekly high assumed in by ~13:30; PM more likely (066), or the London close 10:00-12:00
    (084). Valid once a bearish shift confirms the high (075 ~1:40).
  - Entry by any model (066: PM SB); exits partial at 20%, more at the upper gradient, most at 25%.

A first L20 touch does not end the setup: valid until 16:00, further entries in the PM SB window (14:00-15:00) on retraces into bearish arrays while the week high holds.   - Caption-gap fix (new-htf-regime-trigger-and-entry; cc03_002 ~00:14:23, ~00:22:50, cc03_004 ~00:08:22, ~00:09:54: "if you get the market trade above a down candle you wait for a return back to that down candle"): the HTF activation -> return -> target sequence (also B10). Monthly range = the latest down-close candle D and up-close candle U. Bullish: when a later candle trades above high[D], D is a bullish OB; wait for a return into D and buy at or below its level, refining to the daily / weekly down candle inside it (wicks through are fine while the refined candle's MT holds); target U and the old / equal highs above. Daily alternative: at a monthly bullish OB wait for a break above a daily short-term high, then buy the return. Bearish: at a monthly / weekly bearish OB the move is confirmed when price trades below the low of the last up-close candle; then sell the return into a breaker or the bearish OB or a stop run above an old high; target the sell stops below the bodies, then the HTF bullish OB.   - Caption-gap fix (M17-daily-stop-entry-at-open-with-roll / -limit-at-close / -candle-entry-needs-daily-pd-array / -limit-after-mss; cc05_016 ~00:00:36, ~00:03:05, cc05_017 ~00:00:41, ~00:04:48, ~00:01:25: "if it doesn't trade back above the opening price you don't get a fill you just got to wait for another new down candle"): the daily-candle models (long; shorts mirror). (1) A monthly / weekly PD array above (preferably both) is the draw. (2) A daily candle CLOSES down (ignore it while forming). (3a) Stop model: a BUY STOP at that candle's OPEN; if not filled each new down-close candle moves it to its own open, until the HTF array is reached. (3b) Limit model: a BUY LIMIT at its CLOSE (fills when a later bar trades at least 1 tick through, E3; the order lifetime is unstated - replace it with each new qualifying close [ours]). Prefer setups at or below EQ of the monthly / weekly range; after partials the same size may be re-added on a return to the entry open; the stop is deferred to management. Conflicts with E1 (cc10_011 ~15:32: index buy stop above a down-close candle's HIGH). The first entry needs the daily move against the trade to trade INTO a daily PD array (a bearish OB, a gap, a filled void, a run above a recent daily high, an old daily low retested - mirrored for longs); once the HTF move is underway follow-on candles toward the array are also valid; a bare candle with no PD-array or HTF context is not a setup. Preferred (not a hard filter): the daily has already shown a market structure break higher and is underway toward the HTF draw (continuation entries in the middle of the move). Study only: `bh6t.daily_candle_entries` (`need_array`, `need_mss`).

Study only: `bh6t.sequence_states` / `swing_entries(sequence=True)`.

Long mirrors. Study only: `bh6t.swing_entries` defaults (4h, 378 sessions of arrays, `min_rr` 3).
  - Caption-gap fix (new-htf-ob-mt-entry-4h-ob-confirm; cc06_006 ~00:23:25: "we're using the mean threshold on the monthly candle and it's also the opening of that bullish candle that makes the high"): short - the monthly bearish OB = the last up-close monthly candle before the down move, MT = 50%; when price returns to the MT watch the 4h: a 4h up-close candle runs above the MT and the next 4h candle(s) turn down (a 4h bearish OB); sell limit at that 4h OB candle's open (at / near the monthly MT); stop = the 4h candle high + 1 pip / tick (70 pips in the example); target the first monthly discount array (585 pips, ~8R); require >= 3R.   - Caption-gap fix (M17-osok-short-term-trade-definition; cc07_001 ~00:01:14: "it's the practice of trading a duration of one week or a few days"): One Shot One Kill (OSOK) = a short-term trade held from a few days up to one week; setups are framed on the monthly and weekly charts; trade in the direction of the current or next week's probable range (the weekly range is the backbone); trending and range-bound weeks both qualify; take only clear, obvious setups (they form every week), otherwise sit out. Statistics only (M17): `bh7s.osok` - weekly holding, flat by the Friday close [our interpretation].
  - Caption-gap fix (M17-osok-execution-chain-1h-minimum; cc07_001 ~00:16:17, cc07_006 ~00:20:04: "our one hour chart is our executable time frame for one shot one kills"): bearish monthly frame - scan the weekly, daily and 4h for premium arrays above price (levels plotted on the 4h); when price trades into one refine on the 1h (a 1h array inside the HTF array; breakers / mitigation blocks are not visible on the 4h; a second entry if the 4h array was missed) and sell; target the weekly discount array. No entries below 1h (below 60m = day trading / scalping). 1h arrays alone may be executed inside a kill zone, Mon-Wed (cc07_001 ~31:20). Bullish mirror.

  - Caption-gap fix (M17-osok-weekly-to-daily; cc07_001 ~00:22:41: "this my friends is the one shot one kill setup"): OSOK short - origin = a weekly premium array; objective = an opposing daily discount array (the source does not say "nearest"; our pick is the nearest UNTOUCHED one); list every premium PD array on the weekly, daily and 4h, wait for price to trade into one, sell on the 1h chart and aim for the daily discount array; long is the mirror. A tradeable instance most weeks, all asset classes.
  - Caption-gap fix (new-frequency-by-origin-tf; cc07_001 ~00:18:29: "if it's a weekly or it's a monthly level we could potentially see multiple short-term trades form"): OSOK statistics - a monthly / weekly-origin PD array can yield several sequential short-term setups toward the opposing weekly array while the target is unmet; a 4h-origin frame yields fewer (not a hard cap of one), still high probability. `bh7s.origin_report` counts the signals per leg by origin timeframe.
  - Caption-gap fix (E12-daily-downclose-add-stops; cc05_016 ~00:03:14: "every time you get a new successive down candle you keep adding that new entry at the opening price"): daily model (CC) - with a monthly / weekly PD array as the draw, a buy stop at the open of the latest closed down-close daily candle, rolled forward to the next down-close candle's open until filled; after a fill and a partial, if price retraces to that same opening price put the partial quantity back there; further entries from new down-close candles only while price is at or below HTF equilibrium and has not reached the monthly / weekly premium array. Prefer stocks near weekly old highs (a "low resistance liquidity run"); drop stocks too extended or far under their old highs; express with affordable call options; narrow to 2-4 names. Research only (parked): `stocks_watch.jan_smt`, `stocks_watch.gap_refill` (needs `stocks_mode`).
